CREATE TABLE KFP.API_BOND_INFO
(
    API_KEY                         VARCHAR2(64),
    PDNO                            VARCHAR2(20),
    PRDT_TYPE_CD                    VARCHAR2(3),
    KSD_BOND_ITEM_NAME              VARCHAR2(160),
    KSD_BOND_ITEM_ENG_NAME          VARCHAR2(160),
    KSD_BOND_LSTG_TYPE_CD           VARCHAR2(2),
    KSD_OFRG_DVSN_CD                VARCHAR2(2),
    KSD_BOND_INT_DFRM_DVSN_CD       VARCHAR2(1),
    ISSU_DT                         VARCHAR2(8),
    RDPT_DT                         VARCHAR2(8),
    RVNU_DT                         VARCHAR2(8),
    ISO_CRCY_CD                     VARCHAR2(3),
    MDWY_RDPT_DT                    VARCHAR2(8),
    KSD_RCVG_BOND_DSCT_RT           NUMBER,
    KSD_RCVG_BOND_SRFC_INRT         NUMBER,
    BOND_EXPD_RDPT_RT               NUMBER,
    KSD_PRCA_RDPT_MTHD_CD           VARCHAR2(2),
    INT_CALTM_MCNT                  VARCHAR2(10),
    KSD_INT_CALC_UNIT_CD            VARCHAR2(1),
    UVAL_CUT_DVSN_CD                VARCHAR2(1),
    UVAL_CUT_DCPT_DGIT              NUMBER,
    KSD_DYDV_CALTM_APLY_DVSN_CD     VARCHAR2(2),
    DYDV_CALC_DCNT                  NUMBER,
    BOND_EXPD_ASRC_ERNG_RT          NUMBER,
    PADF_PLAC_HDOF_NAME             VARCHAR2(80),
    LSTG_DT                         VARCHAR2(8),
    LSTG_ABOL_DT                    VARCHAR2(8),
    KSD_BOND_ISSU_MTHD_CD           VARCHAR2(1),
    LAPS_INDF_YN                    VARCHAR2(1),
    KSD_LHDY_PNIA_DFRM_MTHD_CD      VARCHAR2(1),
    FRST_INT_DFRM_DT                VARCHAR2(8),
    KSD_PRCM_LNKG_GVBD_YN           VARCHAR2(1),
    DPSI_END_DT                     VARCHAR2(8),
    DPSI_STRT_DT                    VARCHAR2(8),
    DPSI_PSBL_YN                    VARCHAR2(1),
    ATYP_RDPT_BOND_ERLM_YN          VARCHAR2(1),
    DSHN_OCCR_YN                    VARCHAR2(1),
    EXPD_EXTS_YN                    VARCHAR2(1),
    PCLR_PTCR_TEXT                  VARCHAR2(500),
    DPSI_PSBL_EXCP_STAT_CD          VARCHAR2(8),
    EXPD_EXTS_SRDP_RCNT             NUMBER,
    EXPD_EXTS_SRDP_RT               NUMBER,
    EXPD_RDPT_RT                    NUMBER,
    EXPD_ASRC_ERNG_RT               NUMBER,
    BOND_INT_DFRM_MTHD_CD           VARCHAR2(2),
    INT_DFRM_DAY_TYPE_CD            VARCHAR2(2),
    PRCA_DFMT_TERM_MCNT             NUMBER,
    SPLT_RDPT_RCNT                  NUMBER,
    RGBF_INT_DFRM_DT                VARCHAR2(8),
    NXTM_INT_DFRM_DT                VARCHAR2(8),
    SPRX_PSBL_YN                    VARCHAR2(1),
    ICTX_RT_DVSN_CD                 VARCHAR2(2),
    BOND_CLSF_CD                    VARCHAR2(6),
    BOND_CLSF_KOR_NAME              VARCHAR2(80),
    INT_MNED_DVSN_CD                VARCHAR2(1),
    PNIA_INT_CALC_UNPR              NUMBER,
    FRN_INTR                        NUMBER,
    APLY_DAY_PRCM_IDX_LNKG_CEFC     NUMBER,
    KSD_EXPD_DYDV_CALC_BASS_CD      VARCHAR2(1),
    EXPD_DYDV_CALC_DCNT             NUMBER,
    KSD_CBBW_DVSN_CD                VARCHAR2(1),
    CRFD_ITEM_YN                    VARCHAR2(1),
    PNIA_BANK_OFDY_DFRM_MTHD_CD     VARCHAR2(12),
    QIB_YN                          VARCHAR2(1),
    QIB_CCLC_DT                     VARCHAR2(8),
    CSBD_YN                         VARCHAR2(1),
    CSBD_CCLC_DT                    VARCHAR2(8),
    KSD_OPCB_YN                     VARCHAR2(1),
    KSD_SODN_YN                     VARCHAR2(1),
    KSD_RQDI_SCTY_YN                VARCHAR2(1),
    ELEC_SCTY_YN                    VARCHAR2(1),
    RGHT_ECIS_MBDY_DVSN_CD          VARCHAR2(1),
    INT_RKNG_MTHD_DVSN_CD           VARCHAR2(1),
    OFRG_DVSN_CD                    VARCHAR2(2),
    KSD_TOT_ISSU_AMT                NUMBER,
    NEXT_INDF_CHK_ECLS_YN           VARCHAR2(1),
    KSD_BOND_INTR_DVSN_CD           VARCHAR2(1),
    KSD_INRT_APLY_DVSN_CD           VARCHAR2(1),
    KRX_ISSU_ISTT_CD                VARCHAR2(5),
    KSD_INDF_FRQC_UDER_CALC_CD      VARCHAR2(1),
    KSD_INDF_FRQC_UDER_CALC_DCNT    VARCHAR2(4),
    TLG_RCVG_DTL_DTIME              VARCHAR2(20)
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.API_BOND_INFO_IDX
ON KFP.API_BOND_INFO (API_KEY) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

GRANT SELECT ON KFP.API_BOND_INFO TO AAMS;

CREATE TABLE KFP.API_CFG
(
    COMPANY         VARCHAR2(16) NOT NULL,
    APPKEY          VARCHAR2(256) NOT NULL,
    APPSECRET       VARCHAR2(256) NOT NULL,
    ACCESS_TOKEN    VARCHAR2(512) NOT NULL,
    EXPIRED         VARCHAR2(32) NOT NULL,
    TOKEN_TYPE      VARCHAR2(32) NOT NULL,
    GRANT_TYPE      VARCHAR2(128) NOT NULL,
    EXPIRES_IN      NUMBER NOT NULL,
    ISSUED_AT       NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TOKEN_URL       VARCHAR2(256) NOT NULL,
    LAST_CHECK      VARCHAR2(32)
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.API_CFG_PK
ON KFP.API_CFG (COMPANY,APPSECRET) 
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_CFG IS 'API 인증서';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.COMPANY IS '회사명';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.APPKEY IS 'APP KEY';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.APPSECRET IS 'APP 비밀 KEY';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.ACCESS_TOKEN IS '엑세스 토큰';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.EXPIRED IS '토큰 만료 여부';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.TOKEN_TYPE IS '토큰 유형';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.GRANT_TYPE IS '권한 유형';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.EXPIRES_IN IS '토큰 만료 시간';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.ISSUED_AT IS '토큰 발급 시각';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CFG.TOKEN_URL IS '토큰 발급 URL';

CREATE TABLE KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL
(
    API_KEY VARCHAR2(64) NOT NULL
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_IDX
ON KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL (API_KEY) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);


CREATE TABLE KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB
(
    API_KEY         VARCHAR2(64),
    SEQ             NUMBER,
    EXCH_CD         VARCHAR2(4),
    CLAS_CD         VARCHAR2(4),
    CRC_CD          VARCHAR2(3),
    STTL_PRICE      NUMBER,
    STTL_DATE       VARCHAR2(8),
    TRST_MGN        NUMBER,
    DISP_DIGIT      NUMBER,
    TICK_SZ         NUMBER,
    TICK_VAL        NUMBER,
    MRKT_OPEN_DATE  VARCHAR2(8),
    MRKT_OPEN_TIME  VARCHAR2(6),
    MRKT_CLOSE_DATE VARCHAR2(8),
    MRKT_CLOSE_TIME VARCHAR2(6),
    TRD_FR_DATE     VARCHAR2(8),
    EXPR_DATE       VARCHAR2(8),
    TRD_TO_DATE     VARCHAR2(8),
    REMN_CNT        NUMBER,
    STAT_TP         NUMBER,
    CTRT_SIZE       NUMBER,
    STL_TP          VARCHAR2(100),
    FRST_NOTI_DATE  VARCHAR2(8),
    SUB_EXCH_NM     VARCHAR2(100)
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB_IDX
ON KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB (API_KEY) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB IS '해외선물옵션 기본시세 - 해외선물 상품기본정보';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.EXCH_CD IS '거래소코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.CLAS_CD IS '품목종류';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.CRC_CD IS '거래통화';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.STTL_PRICE IS '정산가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.STTL_DATE IS '정산일';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.TRST_MGN IS '증거금';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.DISP_DIGIT IS '가격표시진법';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.TICK_SZ IS '틱사이즈';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.TICK_VAL IS '틱가치';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.MRKT_OPEN_DATE IS '장개시일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.MRKT_OPEN_TIME IS '장개시시각';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.MRKT_CLOSE_DATE IS '장마감일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_CONTRACT_DETAIL_SUB.MRKT_CLOSE_TIME IS '장마감시각';

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB_I  
    AFTER INSERT ON API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB  
    FOR EACH ROW 
-----------------------
-- 환율 갱신 trigger --
-----------------------
DECLARE
    w_corp_gr                    VARCHAR2(8);
    w_frid                       VARCHAR2(20);
    w_currency                   VARCHAR2(20);
BEGIN
    if NVL (:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'00000000') > '00000000'    then
        BEGIN
            SELECT CORP_GR
                 , KEY1
                 , KEY2
              INTO w_corp_gr
                 , w_frid
                 , w_currency
              FROM API_Q qq
             WHERE qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL
               AND qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY;

            if  w_frid = 'X'    then    -- 환율
                MERGE INTO SYX1HY t1 
                   USING DUAL  ON ( t1.CORP_GR   = w_corp_gr
                                AND t1.GIJUN_YMD = TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'yyyymmdd')
                                AND t1.CURRENCY  = w_currency )
                WHEN MATCHED THEN
                     UPDATE
                        SET USD_GIJUN = :new.OVRS_NMIX_PRPR
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                     INSERT
                     VALUES ( w_corp_gr                                 /* _1- */
                            , TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE, 'yyyymmdd')  /* _2- */
                            , w_currency                                /* _3- */
                            , 0                                         /* _4- */
                            , sysdate                                   /* _5- */
                            , :new.OVRS_NMIX_PRPR                       /* _6- */
                            );
            elsif w_frid = 'SPX'    then    -- S&P500 지수
                MERGE INTO SYT0LP t1 
                   USING DUAL  ON ( t1.CORP_GR = w_corp_gr
                                AND t1.YMD     = TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'yyyymmdd')
                                AND t1.JM_CD   = 'S&P500' )
                WHEN MATCHED THEN
                     UPDATE
                        SET JONGA     = :new.OVRS_NMIX_PRPR
                          , LOAD_TIME = sysdate
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                     INSERT ( CORP_GR    /* _1- */
                            , YMD        /* _2- */
                            , JM_CD      /* _3- */
                            , JONGA      /* _4- */
                            , TVOL       /* _5- */
                            , LOAD_TIME  /* _6- */
                            )
                     VALUES ( w_corp_gr                                 /* _1- */
                            , TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE, 'yyyymmdd')  /* _2- */
                            , 'S&P500'                                  /* _3- */
                            , :new.OVRS_NMIX_PRPR                       /* _4- */
                            , :new.ACML_VOL                             /* _5- */
                            , sysdate                                   /* _6- */
                            );
            elsif w_frid = 'NDX'    then    -- NASDAQ100 지수
                MERGE INTO SYT0LP t1 
                   USING DUAL  ON ( t1.CORP_GR = w_corp_gr
                                AND t1.YMD     = TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'yyyymmdd')
                                AND t1.JM_CD   = 'NASDAQ100' )
                WHEN MATCHED THEN
                     UPDATE
                        SET JONGA     = :new.OVRS_NMIX_PRPR
                          , LOAD_TIME = sysdate
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                     INSERT ( CORP_GR    /* _1- */
                            , YMD        /* _2- */
                            , JM_CD      /* _3- */
                            , JONGA      /* _4- */
                            , TVOL       /* _5- */
                            , LOAD_TIME  /* _6- */
                            )
                     VALUES ( w_corp_gr                                 /* _1- */
                            , TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE, 'yyyymmdd')  /* _2- */
                            , 'NASDAQ100'                               /* _3- */
                            , :new.OVRS_NMIX_PRPR                       /* _4- */
                            , :new.ACML_VOL                             /* _5- */
                            , sysdate                                   /* _6- */
                            );
            end if;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB IS '해외주식 기본시세 - 해외주식 종목 지수 환율 기간별시세(서브)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB.STCK_BSOP_DATE IS '영업일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB.OVRS_NMIX_PRPR IS '현재가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB.OVRS_NMIX_OPRC IS '시가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB.OVRS_NMIX_HGPR IS '최고가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB.OVRS_NMIX_LWPR IS '최저가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB.ACML_VOL IS '누적거래량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_CHARTPRICE_SUB.MOD_YN IS '변경여부';

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB_I  
    AFTER INSERT ON API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB  
    FOR EACH ROW 
------------------------------
-- 선물 일자별 종가 trigger --
------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_jm_cd                      VARCHAR2(20);
    w_cnt                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if NVL (:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'00000000') > '00000000'    then
        BEGIN
            SELECT KEY1
                 , NUM1
              INTO w_jm_cd
                 , w_cnt
              FROM API_Q qq
             WHERE qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL
               AND qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY;

            if  :new.SEQ <= w_cnt OR NVL (w_cnt,0) = 0  then
                MERGE INTO SJT1TG t1 
                   USING DUAL  ON ( t1.YMD       = TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'yyyymmdd')
                                AND t1.KOSCOM_CD = w_jm_cd )
                WHEN MATCHED THEN
                     UPDATE
                        SET OPEN     = :new.FUTS_OPRC
                          , HIGH     = :new.FUTS_HGPR
                          , LOW      = :new.FUTS_LWPR
                          , CLOSE    = :new.FUTS_PRPR
                          , VOLUME   = :new.ACML_VOL
                          , VALUE    = :new.ACML_TR_PBMN
                          , CYBOS_DT = sysdate
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                     INSERT ( YMD        /* _1- */
                            , KOSCOM_CD  /* _2- */
                            , OPEN       /* _3- */
                            , HIGH       /* _4- */
                            , LOW        /* _5- */
                            , CLOSE      /* _6- */
                            , VOLUME     /* _7- */
                            , VALUE      /* _8- */
                            , CYBOS_DT   /* _9- */
                            )
                     VALUES ( TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'yyyymmdd')  /* _1- */
                            , w_jm_cd                                  /* _2- */
                            , :new.FUTS_OPRC                           /* _3- */
                            , :new.FUTS_HGPR                           /* _4- */
                            , :new.FUTS_LWPR                           /* _5- */
                            , :new.FUTS_PRPR                           /* _6- */
                            , :new.ACML_VOL                            /* _7- */
                            , :new.ACML_TR_PBMN                        /* _8- */
                            , sysdate                                  /* _9- */
                            );
            end if;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB.STCK_BSOP_DATE IS '영업일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB.FUTS_PRPR IS '현재가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB.FUTS_OPRC IS '시가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB.FUTS_HGPR IS '최고가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB.FUTS_LWPR IS '최저가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB.ACML_VOL IS '누적 거래량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB.ACML_TR_PBMN IS '누적 거래 대금';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_FUOPCHARTPRICE_SUB.MOD_YN IS '변경 여부';

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB_I  
    AFTER INSERT ON API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB  
    FOR EACH ROW 
------------------------------------
-- 해외주식 과거주가 갱신 trigger --
------------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_corp_gr                    VARCHAR2(8);
    w_jm_cd                      VARCHAR2(20);
    w_cnt                        NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if NVL (:new.XYMD,'00000000') > '00000000' AND :new.TVOL > 0    then
        BEGIN
            SELECT CORP_GR
                 , KEY1
                 , NUM1
              INTO w_corp_gr
                 , w_jm_cd
                 , w_cnt
              FROM API_Q qq
             WHERE qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL
               AND qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY;

            if  :new.SEQ <= w_cnt   then
                MERGE INTO SYT0LP t1 
                   USING DUAL  ON ( t1.CORP_GR = w_corp_gr
                                AND t1.YMD     = TO_DATE(:new.XYMD,'yyyymmdd')
                                AND t1.JM_CD   = w_jm_cd )
                WHEN MATCHED THEN
                     UPDATE
                        SET JONGA     = :new.CLOS
                          , TVOL      = :new.TVOL
                          , TAMT      = :new.TAMT
                          , LOAD_TIME = sysdate
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                     INSERT ( CORP_GR    /* _1- */
                            , YMD        /* _2- */
                            , JM_CD      /* _3- */
                            , JONGA      /* _4- */
                            , TVOL       /* _5- */
                            , TAMT       /* _6- */
                            , LOAD_TIME  /* _7- */
                            )
                     VALUES ( w_corp_gr                      /* _1- */
                            , TO_DATE(:new.XYMD,'yyyymmdd')  /* _2- */
                            , w_jm_cd                        /* _3- */
                            , :new.CLOS                      /* _4- */
                            , :new.TVOL                      /* _5- */
                            , :new.TAMT                      /* _6- */
                            , sysdate                        /* _7- */
                            );
            end if;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB IS '해외주식 기본시세 - 해외주식기간별시세(서브)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.XYMD IS '일자(YYYYMMDD)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.CLOS IS '종가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.SIGN IS '대비기호';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.DIFF IS '대비';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.RATE IS '등락율';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.OPEN IS '시가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.HIGH IS '고가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.LOW IS '저가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.TVOL IS '거래량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.TAMT IS '거래대금';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.PBID IS '매수호가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.VBID IS '매수호가잔량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.PASK IS '매도호가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.VASK IS '매도호가잔량';

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB_I  
    AFTER INSERT ON API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB  
    FOR EACH ROW 
-------------------------------
-- 종합주가지수 갱신 trigger --
-------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_corp_gr                    VARCHAR2(8);
    w_col_id                     VARCHAR2(20);
BEGIN
    if NVL (:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'00000000') > '00000000' AND :new.SEQ = 1   then
        BEGIN
            SELECT CORP_GR
                 , KEY1
              INTO w_corp_gr
                 , w_col_id
              FROM API_Q qq
             WHERE qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL
               AND qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY;

            MERGE INTO SKT0BM t1 
               USING DUAL  ON ( t1.CORP_GR = w_corp_gr
                            AND t1.YMD     = TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'yyyymmdd')
                            AND t1.COL_ID  = w_col_id )
            WHEN MATCHED THEN
                 UPDATE
                    SET COL_VAL = :new.BSTP_NMIX_PRPR
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                 INSERT
                 VALUES ( w_corp_gr                                /* _1- */
                        , TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'yyyymmdd')  /* _2- */
                        , w_col_id                                 /* _3- */
                        , :new.BSTP_NMIX_PRPR                      /* _4- */
                        );
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.STCK_BSOP_DATE IS '주식 영업 일자  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.BSTP_NMIX_PRPR IS '업종 지수 현재가  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.PRDY_VRSS_SIGN IS '전일 대비 부호  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.BSTP_NMIX_PRDY_VRSS IS '업종 지수 전일 대비  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.BSTP_NMIX_PRDY_CTRT IS '업종 지수 전일 대비율  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.BSTP_NMIX_OPRC IS '업종 지수 시가2  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.BSTP_NMIX_HGPR IS '업종 지수 최고가  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.BSTP_NMIX_LWPR IS '업종 지수 최저가  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.ACML_VOL_RLIM IS '누적 거래량 비중  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.ACML_VOL IS '누적 거래량  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.ACML_TR_PBMN IS '누적 거래 대금  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.INVT_NEW_PSDG IS '투자 신 심리도  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INDEX_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.D20_DSRT IS '20일 이격도  ';

    OVRS_STCK_ERLM_ROSN_CD      VARCHAR2(2),
    OVRS_STCK_HIST_RGHT_DVSN_CD VARCHAR2(2),

    MINT_DCPT_TRAD_PSBL_YN      VARCHAR2(1),

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_INFO_I  
    AFTER INSERT  
    ON API_INFO FOR EACH ROW 
--------------------------------
-- 해외 주식정보 갱신 trigger --
--------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_corp_gr                      VARCHAR2(8);
    w_jm_cd                        VARCHAR2(20);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        if  :new.STD_PDNO IS NOT NULL   then
            SELECT CORP_GR
                 , KEY1
              INTO w_corp_gr
                 , w_jm_cd
              FROM API_Q qq  /* API 요청 및 처리결과 */
             WHERE 1 = 1
               AND qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY
               AND qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL;

            UPDATE SYM0YA   /* 해외 자산종목 */
               SET BALH_NATION    = (SELECT NATION_CD
                                       FROM SZX0WA h1  /* 국가코드 */
                                      WHERE 1 = 1
                                        AND API_ID = :new.NATN_CD)
                 , CURRENCY       = :new.TR_CRCY_CD
                 , AEKM           = :new.OVRS_PAPR
                 , SANGJ_YMD      = CASE WHEN NVL(:new.LSTG_DT,'00000000') > '00000000'
                                         THEN TO_DATE (:new.LSTG_DT,'yyyymmdd')
                                         ELSE SANGJ_YMD END
                 , LSY_YMD        = (SELECT NVL(TO_DATE (EXPR_DATE,'yyyymmdd'),LSY_YMD)
                                       FROM SRS_CD N
                                      WHERE 1 = 1
                                        AND srs_cd = w_jm_cd)
                 , SANGJ_JUSU     = :new.LSTG_STCK_NUM
                 , BLBG_TCKR_TEXT = :new.BLBG_TCKR_TEXT
                 , SEDOL          = :new.SEDOL_NO
                 , TR_MKET_CD     = :new.TR_MKET_CD
                 , TR_MKET_NAME   = :new.TR_MKET_NAME
                 , OVRS_EXCG_CD   = :new.OVRS_EXCG_CD
                 , OVRS_EXCG_NAME = :new.OVRS_EXCG_NAME
                 , IU_USER        = 'API_INFO'
                 , IU_DT          = SYSDATE
             WHERE 1 = 1 
               AND CORP_GR = w_corp_gr
               AND JM_CD   = w_jm_cd;
        end if;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        NULL;
    END;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB_I  
    AFTER INSERT ON API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB  
    FOR EACH ROW 
------------------------------
-- 주식 일자별 종가 trigger --
------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_koscom_cd                  VARCHAR2(20);
    w_cnt number;
BEGIN
    if NVL (:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'00000000') > '00000000'    then
        BEGIN
            SELECT KEY1
                 , NUM1
              INTO w_koscom_cd
                 , w_cnt
              FROM API_Q qq
             WHERE qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL
               AND qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY;

            if  :new.SEQ <= w_cnt OR NVL(w_cnt,0)=0 then
                MERGE INTO SJT1TG t1 
                   USING DUAL  ON ( t1.YMD       = TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'yyyymmdd')
                                AND t1.KOSCOM_CD = w_koscom_cd )
                WHEN MATCHED THEN
                     UPDATE
                        SET OPEN     = :new.STCK_OPRC
                          , HIGH     = :new.STCK_HGPR
                          , LOW      = :new.STCK_LWPR
                          , CLOSE    = :new.STCK_CLPR
                          , VOLUME   = :new.ACML_VOL
                          , PRECLOSE = :new.STCK_CLPR - :new.PRDY_VRSS
                          , CHANGE   = :new.PRDY_VRSS
                          , CYBOS_DT = sysdate
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                     INSERT ( YMD        /* _1- */
                            , KOSCOM_CD  /* _2- */
                            , OPEN       /* _3- */
                            , HIGH       /* _4- */
                            , LOW        /* _5- */
                            , CLOSE      /* _6- */
                            , VOLUME     /* _7- */
                            , PRECLOSE   /* _8- */
                            , CHANGE     /* _9- */
                            , CYBOS_DT   /* _10- */
                            )
                     VALUES ( TO_DATE(:new.STCK_BSOP_DATE,'yyyymmdd')  /* _1- */
                            , w_koscom_cd                              /* _2- */
                            , :new.STCK_OPRC                           /* _3- */
                            , :new.STCK_HGPR                           /* _4- */
                            , :new.STCK_LWPR                           /* _5- */
                            , :new.STCK_CLPR                           /* _6- */
                            , :new.ACML_VOL                            /* _7- */
                            , :new.STCK_CLPR - :new.PRDY_VRSS          /* _8- */
                            , :new.PRDY_VRSS                           /* _9- */
                            , sysdate                                  /* _10- */
                            );
            end if;
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.STCK_BSOP_DATE IS '주식 영업 일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.STCK_OPRC IS '주식 시가2';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.STCK_HGPR IS '주식 최고가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.STCK_LWPR IS '주식 최저가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.STCK_CLPR IS '주식 종가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.ACML_VOL IS '누적 거래량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.PRDY_VRSS_VOL_RATE IS '전일 대비 거래량 비율';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.PRDY_VRSS IS '전일 대비';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.PRDY_VRSS_SIGN IS '전일 대비 부호';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.PRDY_CTRT IS '전일 대비율';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.HTS_FRGN_EHRT IS 'HTS 외국인 소진율';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.FRGN_NTBY_QTY IS '외국인 순매수 수량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.FLNG_CLS_CODE IS '락 구분 코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_INQUIRE_DAILY_PRICE_SUB.ACML_PRTT_RATE IS '누적 분할 비율';

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_INQUIRE_PRICE_I  
    AFTER INSERT ON API_INQUIRE_PRICE  
    FOR EACH ROW 
------------------------------------
-- 주식 현재가(종가) 갱신 trigger --
------------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_ymd                        DATE;
    w_koscom_cd                  VARCHAR2(20);
BEGIN
    if NVL (:new.STCK_PRPR,0) > 0   then
        BEGIN
            SELECT TR_YMD
                 , KEY1
              INTO w_ymd
                 , w_koscom_cd
              FROM API_Q qq
             WHERE qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL
               AND qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY;

            MERGE INTO SJT1TG t1 
               USING DUAL  ON ( t1.YMD       = w_ymd
                            AND t1.KOSCOM_CD = w_koscom_cd )
            WHEN MATCHED THEN
                 UPDATE
                    SET OPEN       = :new.STCK_OPRC
                      , HIGH       = :new.STCK_HGPR
                      , LOW        = :new.STCK_LWPR
                      , CLOSE      = :new.STCK_PRPR
                      , VOLUME     = :new.ACML_VOL
                      , VALUE      = :new.ACML_TR_PBMN
                      , PRECLOSE   = :new.STCK_PRPR - :new.PRDY_VRSS
                      , CHANGE     = :new.PRDY_VRSS
                      , UNDER      = :new.MANG_ISSU_CLS_CODE
                      , AEKM       = :new.STCK_FCAM
                      , SANGJ_JUSU = :new.LSTN_STCN
                      , GYUL_MM    = :new.STAC_MONTH
                      , CYBOS_DT   = sysdate
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                 INSERT
                 VALUES ( w_ymd                            /* _1- */
                        , w_koscom_cd                      /* _2- */
                        , :new.STCK_OPRC                   /* _3- */
                        , :new.STCK_HGPR                   /* _4- */
                        , :new.STCK_LWPR                   /* _5- */
                        , :new.STCK_PRPR                   /* _6- */
                        , :new.ACML_VOL                    /* _7- */
                        , :new.ACML_TR_PBMN                /* _8- */
                        , :new.STCK_PRPR - :new.PRDY_VRSS  /* _9- */
                        , :new.PRDY_VRSS                   /* _10- */
                        , :new.MANG_ISSU_CLS_CODE          /* _11- */
                        , :new.STCK_FCAM                   /* _12- */
                        , :new.LSTN_STCN                   /* _13- */
                        , :new.STAC_MONTH                  /* _14- */
                        , sysdate                          /* _15- */
                        , NULL                             /* _16- */
                        , NULL                             /* _17- */
                        );
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_OPT_DAILY_SUB_I  
    AFTER INSERT ON API_OPT_DAILY_SUB  
    FOR EACH ROW 
----------------------------------
-- 파생 콜/풋 종가 갱신 trigger --
----------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_corp_gr                    VARCHAR2(8);
    w_tr_ymd                     DATE;
    w_jm_cd                      VARCHAR2(20);
    w_hangsa_ga                  NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if NVL (:new.DATA_DATE,'00000000') > '00000000' then
        BEGIN
            SELECT CORP_GR
                 , TR_YMD
                 , KEY1
                 , NUM1
              INTO w_corp_gr
                 , w_tr_ymd
                 , w_jm_cd
                 , w_hangsa_ga
              FROM API_Q qq
             WHERE qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL
               AND qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY;

            MERGE INTO SYT0LP t1 
               USING DUAL  ON ( t1.CORP_GR = w_corp_gr
                            AND t1.YMD     = TO_DATE(:new.DATA_DATE,'yyyymmdd')
                            AND t1.JM_CD   = w_jm_cd )
            WHEN MATCHED THEN
                 UPDATE
                    SET JONGA     = :new.LAST_PRICE
                      , HANGSA_GA = w_hangsa_ga
                      , LOAD_TIME = sysdate
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                 INSERT ( CORP_GR    /* _1- */
                        , YMD        /* _2- */
                        , JM_CD      /* _3- */
                        , JONGA      /* _4- */
                        , HANGSA_GA  /* _5- */
                        , TVOL       /* _6- */
                        , LOAD_TIME  /* _7- */
                        )
                 VALUES ( w_corp_gr                           /* _1- */
                        , TO_DATE(:new.DATA_DATE,'yyyymmdd')  /* _2- */
                        , w_jm_cd                             /* _3- */
                        , :new.LAST_PRICE                     /* _4- */
                        , w_hangsa_ga                         /* _5- */
                        , :new.VOL                            /* _6- */
                        , sysdate                             /* _7- */
                        );
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB IS '해외옵션 체결추이(주간)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.SEQ IS '순번';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.DATA_DATE IS '일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.DATA_TIME IS '시간';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.OPEN_PRICE IS '시가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.HIGH_PRICE IS '고가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.LOW_PRICE IS '저가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.LAST_PRICE IS '종가(체결가격)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.LAST_QNTT IS '체결수량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.VOL IS '누적거래수량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.PREV_DIFF_FLAG IS '전일대비구분';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.PREV_DIFF_PRICE IS '전일대비가격';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DAILY_SUB.PREV_DIFF_RATE IS '전일대비율';

ON KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB (API_KEY,SEQ) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB_I  
    AFTER INSERT  
    ON API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB FOR EACH ROW 
--------------------------------
-- 해외 파생정보 갱신 trigger --
--------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_corp_gr                      VARCHAR2(8);
    w_jm_cd                        VARCHAR2(20);
    w_jm_nm                        VARCHAR2(200);
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        if  :new.EXCH_CD IS NOT NULL    then
            SELECT CORP_GR
                 , KEY1
                 , KEY2
              INTO w_corp_gr
                 , w_jm_cd
                 , w_jm_nm
              FROM API_Q qq  /* API 요청 및 처리결과 */
             WHERE qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY
               AND qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL;

            if  w_jm_nm = 'SRS_CD'  then
                UPDATE SRS_CD
                   SET EXCH_CD     = :new.EXCH_CD
                     , CRC_CD      = :new.CRC_CD
                     , EXPR_DATE   = :new.EXPR_DATE
                     , TRD_FR_DATE = :new.TRD_FR_DATE
                     , TRD_TO_DATE = :new.TRD_TO_DATE
                 WHERE SRS_CD = w_jm_cd;
            end if;
        end if;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
        NULL;
    END;
END;
/

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB IS '해외옵션 상품기본정보';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.EXCH_CD IS '거래소코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.CLAS_CD IS '품목종류';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.CRC_CD IS '거래통화';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.STTL_PRICE IS '정산가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.STTL_DATE IS '정산일  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.TRST_MGN IS '증거금  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.DISP_DIGIT IS '가격표시진법  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.TICK_SZ IS '틱사이즈  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.TICK_VAL IS '틱가치  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_DETAIL_SUB.MRKT_OPEN_DATE IS '장개시일자  ';

    PREV_DIFF_PRICE NUMBER,
    PREV_DIFF_RATE  NUMBER,
    BID_QNTT        NUMBER,
    BID_PRICE       NUMBER,
    ASK_QNTT        NUMBER,
    ASK_PRICE       NUMBER,
    PREV_PRICE      NUMBER,

    CRC_CD          VARCHAR2(3),
    TRD_FR_DATE     VARCHAR2(8),
    EXPR_DATE       VARCHAR2(8),
    TRD_TO_DATE     VARCHAR2(8),
    REMN_CNT        NUMBER,
    LAST_QNTT       NUMBER,
    TOT_ASK_QNTT    NUMBER,
    TOT_BID_QNTT    NUMBER,
    TICK_SIZE       NUMBER,

    CLOSE_DATE      VARCHAR2(8),

    INITIAL 64K

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_OPT_PRICE_I  
    AFTER INSERT  
    ON API_OPT_PRICE FOR EACH ROW 
------------------------------------
-- 파생 콜/풋 현재가 갱신 trigger --
------------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_corp_gr                      VARCHAR2(8);
    w_jm_cd                        VARCHAR2(20);
    w_blbg_tckr                    VARCHAR(20);
    w_isin_cd                      VARCHAR(24);
    w_hangsa_ga                    NUMBER;
BEGIN
    if NVL (:new.SBSNSDATE,'00000000') > '00000000' then
        BEGIN
            SELECT CORP_GR
                 , KEY1
                 , KEY2
                 , KEY3
                 , NUM1
              INTO w_corp_gr
                 , w_jm_cd
                 , w_blbg_tckr
                 , w_isin_cd
                 , w_hangsa_ga
              FROM API_Q qq  /* API 요청 및 처리결과 */
             WHERE qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY
               AND qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL;

            MERGE INTO SYT0LP t1  /* 해외수신 종목 시세 */
               USING DUAL ON ( t1.CORP_GR = w_corp_gr
                           AND t1.YMD     = TO_DATE(:new.SBSNSDATE,'yyyymmdd')
                           AND t1.JM_CD   = w_jm_cd )
            WHEN MATCHED THEN
                 UPDATE
                    SET JONGA        = CASE WHEN :new.LAST_PRICE IS NOT NULL THEN :new.LAST_PRICE ELSE JONGA    END
                      , CURRENCY     = CASE WHEN :new.CRC_CD IS NOT NULL     THEN :new.CRC_CD     ELSE CURRENCY END
                      , BLBG_TCKR    = w_blbg_tckr
                      , ISIN_CD      = w_isin_cd
                      , HANGSA_GA    = w_hangsa_ga
                      , BID          = CASE WHEN :new.BID_PRICE IS NOT NULL    THEN :new.BID_PRICE     ELSE BID          END
                      , ASK          = CASE WHEN :new.ASK_PRICE IS NOT NULL    THEN :new.ASK_PRICE     ELSE ASK          END
                      , TIME_GA      = CASE WHEN :new.OPEN_PRICE IS NOT NULL   THEN :new.OPEN_PRICE    ELSE TIME_GA      END
                      , TRD_TO_DATE  = CASE WHEN :new.TRD_TO_DATE IS NOT NULL  THEN :new.TRD_TO_DATE   ELSE TRD_TO_DATE  END
                      , TVOL         = CASE WHEN :new.VOL IS NOT NULL          THEN :new.VOL           ELSE TVOL         END
                      , PX_CLOSE_1D  = CASE WHEN :new.PREV_PRICE IS NOT NULL   THEN :new.PREV_PRICE    ELSE PX_CLOSE_1D  END
                      , TOT_ASK_QNTT = CASE WHEN :new.TOT_ASK_QNTT IS NOT NULL THEN :new.TOT_ASK_QNTT  ELSE TOT_ASK_QNTT END
                      , TOT_BID_QNTT = CASE WHEN :new.TOT_BID_QNTT IS NOT NULL THEN :new.TOT_BID_QNTT  ELSE TOT_BID_QNTT END
                      , LOAD_TIME    = sysdate
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                 INSERT ( CORP_GR       /* _1- */
                        , YMD           /* _2- */
                        , JM_CD         /* _3- */
                        , JONGA         /* _4- */
                        , CURRENCY      /* _5- */
                        , BLBG_TCKR     /* _6- */
                        , ISIN_CD       /* _7- */
                        , HANGSA_GA     /* _8- */
                        , BID           /* _9- */
                        , ASK           /* _10- */
                        , TIME_GA       /* _11- */
                        , TVOL          /* _12- */
                        , PX_CLOSE_1D   /* _13- */
                        , TOT_ASK_QNTT  /* _14- */
                        , TOT_BID_QNTT  /* _15- */
                        , TRD_TO_DATE   /* _16- */
                        , LOAD_TIME     /* _17- */
                        )
                 VALUES ( w_corp_gr                           /* _1- */
                        , TO_DATE(:new.SBSNSDATE,'yyyymmdd')  /* _2- */
                        , w_jm_cd                             /* _3- */
                        , :new.LAST_PRICE                     /* _4- */
                        , :new.CRC_CD                         /* _5- */
                        , w_blbg_tckr                         /* _6- */
                        , w_isin_cd                           /* _7- */
                        , w_hangsa_ga                         /* _8- */
                        , :new.BID_PRICE                      /* _9- */
                        , :new.ASK_PRICE                      /* _10- */
                        , :new.OPEN_PRICE                     /* _11- */
                        , :new.VOL                            /* _12- */
                        , :new.PREV_PRICE                     /* _13- */
                        , :new.TOT_ASK_QNTT                   /* _14- */
                        , :new.TOT_BID_QNTT                   /* _15- */
                        , :new.TRD_TO_DATE                    /* _16- */
                        , sysdate                             /* _17- */
                        );
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN 
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_OPT_PRICE IS '해외선물옵션 기본시세 - 해외옵션종목현재가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.PROC_DATE IS '최종처리일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.PROC_TIME IS '최종처리시각';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.OPEN_PRICE IS '시가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.HIGH_PRICE IS '고가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.LOW_PRICE IS '저가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.LAST_PRICE IS '현재가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.VOL IS '누적거래수량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.PREV_DIFF_FLAG IS '전일대비구분';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.PREV_DIFF_PRICE IS '전일대비가격';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.PREV_DIFF_RATE IS '전일대비율';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.BID_QNTT IS '매수1수량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.BID_PRICE IS '매수1호가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.ASK_QNTT IS '매도1수량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.ASK_PRICE IS '매도1호가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.PREV_PRICE IS '전일종가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.TRST_MGN IS '증거금';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.EXCH_CD IS '거래소코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.CRC_CD IS '거래통화';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.TRD_FR_DATE IS '상장일';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.EXPR_DATE IS '만기일';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.TRD_TO_DATE IS '최종거래일';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.REMN_CNT IS '잔존일수';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.LAST_QNTT IS '체결량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.TOT_ASK_QNTT IS '총매도잔량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.TOT_BID_QNTT IS '총매수잔량';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.TICK_SIZE IS '틱사이즈';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.OPEN_DATE IS '장개시일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.OPEN_TIME IS '장개시시각';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.CLOSE_DATE IS '장종료일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.CLOSE_TIME IS '장종료시각';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.SBSNSDATE IS '영업일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_OPT_PRICE.STTL_PRICE IS '정산가 정산가';

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_PRICE_DETAIL_I  
    AFTER INSERT ON API_PRICE_DETAIL  
    FOR EACH ROW 
----------------------------------
-- 해외주식 현재가 갱신 trigger --
----------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_corp_gr                    VARCHAR2(8);
    w_tr_ymd                     DATE;
    w_jm_cd                      VARCHAR2(20);
BEGIN
    if NVL (:new.LAST,0) > 0    then
        BEGIN
            SELECT CORP_GR
                 , TR_YMD
                 , KEY1
              INTO w_corp_gr
                 , w_tr_ymd
                 , w_jm_cd
              FROM API_Q qq
             WHERE qq.KEY1 IS NOT NULL
               AND qq.API_KEY = :new.API_KEY;

            /* JONGA, TVOL, TAMT 는 API_DAILYPRICE_SUB에서 처리 */
            MERGE INTO SYT0LP t1 
               USING DUAL  ON ( t1.CORP_GR = w_corp_gr
                            AND t1.YMD     = w_tr_ymd
                            AND t1.JM_CD   = w_jm_cd )
            WHEN MATCHED THEN
                 UPDATE
                    SET CURRENCY  = :new.CURR
                      , SHAR      = :new.SHAR
                      , TOMV      = :new.TOMV
                      , T_RATE    = CASE WHEN :new.T_RATE > 0 THEN :new.T_RATE
                                                              ELSE T_RATE END
                      , LOAD_TIME = sysdate
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                 INSERT ( CORP_GR    /* _1- */
                        , YMD        /* _2- */
                        , JM_CD      /* _3- */
                        , JONGA      /* _4- */
                        , CURRENCY   /* _5- */
                        , TVOL       /* _6- */
                        , TAMT       /* _7- */
                        , SHAR       /* _8- */
                        , TOMV       /* _9- */
                        , T_RATE     /* _10- */
                        , LOAD_TIME  /* _11- */
                        )
                 VALUES ( w_corp_gr    /* _1- */
                        , w_tr_ymd     /* _2- */
                        , w_jm_cd      /* _3- */
                        , :new.LAST    /* _4- */
                        , :new.CURR    /* _5- */
                        , :new.TVOL    /* _6- */
                        , :new.TAMT    /* _7- */
                        , :new.SHAR    /* _8- */
                        , :new.TOMV    /* _9- */
                        , :new.T_RATE  /* _10- */
                        , sysdate      /* _11- */
                        );
        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS then
            NULL;
        END;
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_PRICE_DETAIL IS '해외선물옵션 기본시세 - 해외선물종목현재가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_PRICE_DETAIL.RSYM IS '실시간조회종목코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_PRICE_DETAIL.ZDIV IS '소수점자리수';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_PRICE_DETAIL.CURR IS '통화';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_PRICE_DETAIL.VNIT IS '매매단위';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_PRICE_DETAIL.OPEN IS '시가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_PRICE_DETAIL.HIGH IS '고가';

CREATE TABLE KFP.API_Q
(
    COMPANY         VARCHAR2(16),
    URL             VARCHAR2(256),
    HEADERS         VARCHAR2(512),
    QUERYPARAMS     VARCHAR2(2048),
    TABLENAME       VARCHAR2(128),
    SUBTABLE        VARCHAR2(128),
    KEY_VALUE       VARCHAR2(100),
    SUB_KEY_VALUE   VARCHAR2(100),
    API_KEY         VARCHAR2(64),
    REQUEST         VARCHAR2(1) DEFAULT '0',
    CREATED_AT      TIMESTAMP(6) DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    STATUS          NUMBER DEFAULT 0,
    STATUSTEXT      VARCHAR2(128),
    RT_CD           VARCHAR2(1),
    MSG_CD          VARCHAR2(32),
    MSG1            VARCHAR2(256),
    ERRCODE         VARCHAR2(256),
    UPDATED_AT      TIMESTAMP(6) DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CORP_GR         VARCHAR2(8),
    TR_YMD          DATE,
    KEY1            VARCHAR2(40),
    KEY2            VARCHAR2(80),
    KEY3            VARCHAR2(30),
    KEY4            VARCHAR2(40),
    NUM1            NUMBER,
    PG_NM           VARCHAR2(100) DEFAULT 'AUTO'
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

ON KFP.API_Q (API_KEY) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

GRANT SELECT ON KFP.API_Q TO AAMS;
GRANT INSERT ON KFP.API_Q TO AAMS;

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_Q IS 'API 요청 및 처리결과';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_Q.REQUEST IS '0: 요청 1: 완료 2: Error';

CREATE SYNONYM AAMS.API_Q
FOR KFP.API_Q;

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_API_STOCK_INFO_I  
    AFTER INSERT  
    ON API_STOCK_INFO FOR EACH ROW 
------------------------------------------
-- 국내주식/ 발행기관 정보 갱신 trigger --
------------------------------------------
DECLARE
    w_corp_gr                      VARCHAR2(8);
    w_tr_ymd                       DATE;
    w_jm_cd                        VARCHAR2(20);
    w_danc_gb                      VARCHAR2(1);
    w_sosok_gb                     VARCHAR2(1);
BEGIN
    -- 워런트 자료 PASS
    if  :new.STD_PDNO IS NOT NULL AND :new.SCTY_GRP_ID_CD <> 'EW'   then
        if  :new.SCTY_GRP_ID_CD IN ('EF','EN')  then
            w_danc_gb := 'G';
            if  :new.SCTY_GRP_ID_CD = 'EF'  then
                    w_sosok_gb := '3';
            else    w_sosok_gb := 'G';
            end if;
        else
            if  :new.EXCG_DVSN_CD = '03'    then
                w_danc_gb  := 'C';
                w_sosok_gb := 'C';

            elsif :new.EXCG_DVSN_CD = '04'  then
                w_danc_gb  := 'D';
                w_sosok_gb := 'D';
            else
                w_danc_gb  := 'A';
                w_sosok_gb := '1';
            end if;
        end if;

        MERGE INTO SJM0JJ t1  /* 주식종목 */
           USING DUAL ON ( t1.JM_CD = :new.STD_PDNO )
        WHEN MATCHED THEN
             UPDATE
                SET JJ_FNM                = :new.PRDT_NAME120
                  , JJ_NM                 = :new.PRDT_ABRV_NAME
                  , JJ_ENM                = :new.PRDT_ENG_NAME
                  , BALH_GA               = :new.ISSU_PRIC
                  , SANGJ_YMD             = CASE WHEN NVL(:new.SCTS_MKET_LSTG_DT,'00000000') > '00000000'      THEN TO_DATE (:new.SCTS_MKET_LSTG_DT,'yyyymmdd')
                                                 WHEN NVL(:new.KOSDAQ_MKET_LSTG_DT,'00000000') > '00000000'    THEN TO_DATE (:new.KOSDAQ_MKET_LSTG_DT,'yyyymmdd')
                                                 WHEN NVL(:new.FRBD_MKET_LSTG_ABOL_DT,'00000000') > '00000000' THEN TO_DATE (:new.FRBD_MKET_LSTG_ABOL_DT,'yyyymmdd')
                                                                                                               ELSE SANGJ_YMD END
                  , ABOL_DT               = CASE WHEN NVL(:new.SCTS_MKET_LSTG_ABOL_DT,'00000000') > '00000000'   THEN TO_DATE (:new.SCTS_MKET_LSTG_ABOL_DT,'yyyymmdd')
                                                 WHEN NVL(:new.KOSDAQ_MKET_LSTG_ABOL_DT,'00000000') > '00000000' THEN TO_DATE (:new.KOSDAQ_MKET_LSTG_ABOL_DT,'yyyymmdd')
                                                 WHEN NVL(:new.FRBD_MKET_LSTG_ABOL_DT,'00000000') > '00000000'   THEN TO_DATE (:new.FRBD_MKET_LSTG_ABOL_DT,'yyyymmdd')
                                                                                                                 ELSE ABOL_DT END
                  , SANGJ_JUSU            = :new.LSTG_STQT
                  , KOSPIGUBUN            = CASE WHEN NVL(:new.KOSPI200_ITEM_YN,'N')='Y' THEN '2'
                                                                                         ELSE KOSPIGUBUN END
                  , UNDER                 = :new.ADMN_ITEM_YN
                  , STD_IDST_CLSF_CD      = :new.STD_IDST_CLSF_CD
                  , STD_IDST_CLSF_CD_NAME = :new.STD_IDST_CLSF_CD_NAME
                  , STCK_KIND_CD          = :new.STCK_KIND_CD
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
             INSERT ( JM_CD                  /* _1- */
                    , KOSCOM_CD              /* _2- */
                    , JJ_FNM                 /* _3- */
                    , JJ_NM                  /* _4- */
                    , JJ_ENM                 /* _5- */
                    , BALH_CO                /* _6- */
                    , DANC_GB                /* _7- */
                    , WOOS_ILBAN_GB          /* _8- */
                    , CHG_GB                 /* _9- */
                    , NEW_OLD_GB             /* _10- */
                    , BALH_GA                /* _11- */
                    , SANGJ_YMD              /* _12- */
                    , SANGJ_JUSU             /* _13- */
                    , CREATED_YMD            /* _14- */
                    , DEL_YN                 /* _15- */
                    , KOSPIGUBUN             /* _16- */
                    , UNDER                  /* _17- */
                    , STD_IDST_CLSF_CD       /* _18- */
                    , STD_IDST_CLSF_CD_NAME  /* _19- */
                    , STCK_KIND_CD           /* _20- */
                    )
             VALUES ( :new.STD_PDNO               /* _1- */
                    , SUBSTR(:new.PDNO,7,6)       /* _2- */
                    , :new.PRDT_NAME120           /* _3- */
                    , :new.PRDT_ABRV_NAME         /* _4- */
                    , :new.PRDT_ENG_NAME          /* _5- */
                    , :new.ISSU_ISTT_CD           /* _6- */
                    , w_danc_gb                   /* _7- */
                    , SUBSTR(:new.STD_PDNO,9,1)   /* _8- */
                    , SUBSTR(:new.STD_PDNO,10,1)  /* _9- */
                    , SUBSTR(:new.STD_PDNO,11,1)  /* _10- */
                    , :new.ISSU_PRIC              /* _11- */
                    , CASE WHEN NVL(:new.SCTS_MKET_LSTG_DT,'00000000') > '00000000'   THEN TO_DATE (:new.SCTS_MKET_LSTG_DT,'yyyymmdd')
                           WHEN NVL(:new.KOSDAQ_MKET_LSTG_DT,'00000000') > '00000000' THEN TO_DATE (:new.KOSDAQ_MKET_LSTG_DT,'yyyymmdd')
                                                                                      ELSE NULL
                      END                         /* _12- */
                    , :new.LSTG_STQT              /* _13- */
                    , TRUNC(sysdate,'dd')         /* _14- */
                    , 'N'                         /* _15- */
                    , CASE WHEN NVL(:new.KOSPI200_ITEM_YN,'N')='Y' THEN '2'
                                                                   ELSE NULL
                      END                         /* _16- */
                    , :new.ADMN_ITEM_YN           /* _17- */
                    , :new.STD_IDST_CLSF_CD       /* _18- */
                    , :new.STD_IDST_CLSF_CD_NAME  /* _19- */
                    , :new.STCK_KIND_CD           /* _20- */
                    );

        if  :new.PDNO LIKE '%0' then
            MERGE INTO SJX0JB t1  /* 발행기관 */
               USING DUAL ON ( t1.BALH_CO = :new.ISSU_ISTT_CD )
            WHEN MATCHED THEN
                 UPDATE
                    SET TR_STOP_GB = CASE WHEN NVL(:new.LSTG_ABOL_DT,'00000000') > '00000000' THEN '9'
                                          WHEN NVL(:new.TR_STOP_YN,'N')='Y'                   THEN '2'
                                                                                              ELSE '1' END
                      , AEKM       = :new.PAPR
                      , GYUL_MM    = SUBSTR(:new.SETL_MMDD,1,2)
                      , DEL_YN     = 'N'
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                 INSERT ( BALH_CO      /* _1- */
                        , BALH_NM      /* _2- */
                        , TR_STOP_GB   /* _3- */
                        , SOSOK_GB     /* _4- */
                        , AEKM         /* _5- */
                        , GYUL_MM      /* _6- */
                        , BALH_NATION  /* _7- */
                        , DEL_YN       /* _8- */
                        )
                 VALUES ( :new.ISSU_ISTT_CD           /* _1- */
                        , :new.PRDT_ABRV_NAME         /* _2- */
                        , CASE WHEN NVL(:new.LSTG_ABOL_DT,'00000000') > '00000000' THEN '9'
                               WHEN NVL(:new.TR_STOP_YN,'N')='Y'                   THEN '2'
                                                                                   ELSE '1'
                          END                         /* _3- */
                        , w_sosok_gb                  /* _4- */
                        , :new.PAPR                   /* _5- */
                        , SUBSTR(:new.SETL_MMDD,1,2)  /* _6- */
                        , 'KR'                        /* _7- */
                        , 'N'                         /* _8- */
                        );
        end if;
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.API_STOCK_INFO IS '국내주식기본정보';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_STOCK_INFO.PDNO IS '상품번호  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_STOCK_INFO.PRDT_TYPE_CD IS '상품유형코드  ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_STOCK_INFO.MKET_ID_CD IS '시장ID : STK.유가증권
  AGR.농축산물파생, BON.채권파생,   CMD.일반상품시장,
  CUR.통화파생,       ENG.에너지파생, EQU.주식파생,
  ETF.ETF파생,          IRT.금리파생,      KNX.코넥스,
  KSQ.코스닥,          MTL.금속파생,    SPI.주가지수파생, ';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_STOCK_INFO.SCTY_GRP_ID_CD IS '증권그룹ID
  BC.수익증권,      DR.주식예탁증서, EF.ETF,        EN.ETN,  EW.ELW
  FE.해외ETF,        FO.선물옵션,       FS.외국주권, FU.선물, FX.플렉스선물
  GD.금현물,        IC.투자계약증권,  IF.사회간접자본투융자회사
  KN.코넥스주권,  MF.투자회사,       OP.옵션,      RT.부동산투자회사
  SC.선박투자회사, SR.신주인수권증서,  ST.주권, SW.신주인수권증권
  TC.신탁수익증권';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.API_STOCK_INFO.EXCG_DVSN_CD IS '거래소
  01.한국증권, 02.증권거래소, 03.코스닥, 04.K-OTC, 05.선물거래소, 06.CME, 07.EUREX
  21.금현물, 50.미국주간, 51.홍콩, 52.상해B, 53.심천, 54.홍콩거래소, 55.미국, 56.일본,
  57.상해A, 58.심천A, 59.베트남, 61.장전시간외시장, 64.경쟁대량매매, 65.경매매시장,

CREATE TABLE KFP.BSS
(
    CORP_GR     VARCHAR2(8) NOT NULL,
    RUN_DATE    DATE,
    RUN_NU      NUMBER(13),
    RUN_GU      VARCHAR2(4),
    RUN_WHERE   VARCHAR2(800),
    RUN_NAYE    VARCHAR2(3000)
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.BSS.RUN_NAYE IS 'Batch 작업결과 메세지';

)

    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS

(
    STORAGE
    (
        INITIAL 64K
        NEXT 1M
    )
    CHUNK 8192
    RETENTION
);

CREATE TABLE KFP.FO_COM_CODE
(
    COM_TYPE        VARCHAR2(1),
    INFO_TYPE       VARCHAR2(1),
    SHRN_ISCD       VARCHAR2(9),
    STND_ISCD       VARCHAR2(12),
    KOR_NAME        VARCHAR2(120),
    ATM_CLS_CODE    VARCHAR2(1),
    ACPR            NUMBER,
    MMSC_CLS_CODE   VARCHAR2(1),
    PROD_NO         VARCHAR2(3),
    PROD_NAME       VARCHAR2(80)
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.FO_COM_CODE_PK
ON KFP.FO_COM_CODE (COM_TYPE,INFO_TYPE,STND_ISCD) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.FO_COM_CODE.STND_ISCD IS '표준코드';

CREATE TABLE KFP.FW_VERSION
(
    LIB_DIR     VARCHAR2(512) NOT NULL,
    LIB_ID      VARCHAR2(512) NOT NULL,
    LIB_VER     VARCHAR2(8),
    MOD_YMDT    DATE,
    MOD_USER    VARCHAR2(20),
    LIB_CMNT    VARCHAR2(100),
    LIB_FILE    BLOB,
    FILE_SIZE   NUMBER,
    COMPRESSION VARCHAR2(1) DEFAULT '0'
)
TABLESPACE LOB_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS
LOB (LIB_FILE) STORE AS BASICFILE 
(
    STORAGE
    (
        INITIAL 64K
        NEXT 1M
    )
    CHUNK 8192
    RETENTION
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.FW_VERSION_PK
ON KFP.FW_VERSION (LIB_DIR,LIB_ID) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.FW_VERSION IS 'AAMS Version관리';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.FW_VERSION.LIB_DIR IS 'Library Directory';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.FW_VERSION.LIB_ID IS 'Library ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.FW_VERSION.LIB_VER IS 'Library Version(YYYYnnnn)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.FW_VERSION.MOD_YMDT IS '수정일시';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.FW_VERSION.MOD_USER IS '수정자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.FW_VERSION.LIB_CMNT IS '파일설명';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.FW_VERSION.LIB_FILE IS 'Library file';

)

    NEXT 1M
);

CREATE TABLE KFP.HISTORY
(
    SEBU_CD     VARCHAR2(3) NOT NULL,
    YMD         DATE NOT NULL,
    CLIENT      VARCHAR2(100),
    BASIS       VARCHAR2(100),
    TITLE       VARCHAR2(300),
    BFNAME      VARCHAR2(50),
    FEXP        VARCHAR2(4),
    ORG_FNAME   VARCHAR2(400)
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

CREATE INDEX KFP.HISTORY_IDX
ON KFP.HISTORY (YMD,SEBU_CD) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.HISTORY_PK
ON KFP.HISTORY (BFNAME) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.HISTORY IS '시스템별 작업 이력관리';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY.SEBU_CD IS '이력구분[HIS]';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY.YMD IS '발생일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY.CLIENT IS '요청기관(부서)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY.BASIS IS '관련근거(자)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY.TITLE IS '제목';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY.BFNAME IS 'BLOB 파일명';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY.FEXP IS '자료파일 확장자';

CREATE TABLE KFP.HISTORY_BLOB
(
    BFNAME  VARCHAR2(50) NOT NULL,
    BLOB_F  BLOB,
    CLOB_S  CLOB
)
TABLESPACE LOB_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS
LOB (BLOB_F) STORE AS BASICFILE 
(
    STORAGE
    (
        INITIAL 64K
        NEXT 1M
    )
    CHUNK 8192
    RETENTION
)
LOB (CLOB_S) STORE AS BASICFILE 
(
    STORAGE
    (
        INITIAL 64K
        NEXT 1M
    )
    CHUNK 8192
    RETENTION
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.HISTORY_BLOB_PK
ON KFP.HISTORY_BLOB (BFNAME) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

ALTER TABLE KFP.HISTORY_BLOB
ADD CONSTRAINT HISTORY_BLOB_PK PRIMARY KEY (BFNAME);

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.HISTORY_BLOB IS '상세작업내용';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY_BLOB.BFNAME IS 'BLOB 파일명';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY_BLOB.BLOB_F IS '자료파일';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.HISTORY_BLOB.CLOB_S IS '작업내용 TEXT';

CREATE TABLE KFP.PLAN_TABLE
(
    STATEMENT_ID        VARCHAR2(30),
    TIMESTAMP           DATE,
    REMARKS             VARCHAR2(80),
    OPERATION           VARCHAR2(30),
    OPTIONS             VARCHAR2(255),
    OBJECT_NODE         VARCHAR2(128),
    OBJECT_OWNER        VARCHAR2(30),
    OBJECT_NAME         VARCHAR2(30),
    OBJECT_INSTANCE     NUMBER,
    OBJECT_TYPE         VARCHAR2(30),
    OPTIMIZER           VARCHAR2(255),
    SEARCH_COLUMNS      NUMBER,
    ID                  NUMBER,
    PARENT_ID           NUMBER,
    POSITION            NUMBER,
    COST                NUMBER,
    CARDINALITY         NUMBER,
    BYTES               NUMBER,
    OTHER_TAG           VARCHAR2(255),
    PARTITION_START     VARCHAR2(255),
    PARTITION_STOP      VARCHAR2(255),
    PARTITION_ID        NUMBER,
    OTHER               LONG,
    DISTRIBUTION        VARCHAR2(30),
    CPU_COST            NUMBER,
    IO_COST             NUMBER,
    TEMP_SPACE          NUMBER,
    ACCESS_PREDICATES   VARCHAR2(4000),
    FILTER_PREDICATES   VARCHAR2(4000),
    PROJECTION          VARCHAR2(4000),
    TIME                NUMBER,
    QBLOCK_NAME         VARCHAR2(30)
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 64K
)
NOCOMPRESS;

(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)

(
    STORAGE
    (
        INITIAL 64K
        NEXT 1M
    )
    CHUNK 8192
    RETENTION
)

(
    STORAGE
    (
        INITIAL 64K

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.dt_skt0bu_d
    after delete on skt0bu
    for each row
----------------------------------------------
-- 분개장(delete시) 총계정원장 갱신 trigger --
----------------------------------------------
declare
    w_msg                       varchar(200);
begin
    ----------------------------------------------
    --  해당계정의 총계정원장  차대변금액 갱신  --
    ----------------------------------------------
    SR_UPDAG (:old.corp_gr, :old.tr_ymd, :old.work_gb, :old.fund_cd, :old.fund_currency, :old.gwamok, :old.chadae_gb, -1, :old.aek, w_msg);
    if  w_msg != 'Y'    then raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
    end if;
    ---------------------------------------------
    --  합산계정의 총계정원장 차대변금액 갱신  --
    ---------------------------------------------
    for r1 in (
        SELECT  hab_gwamok
              , yonsan * -1  AS yonsan
        FROM    szx0cc t1
        WHERE   gwamok = :old.gwamok
    ) loop
        SR_UPDAG (:old.corp_gr, :old.tr_ymd, :old.work_gb, :old.fund_cd, :old.fund_currency, r1.hab_gwamok, :old.chadae_gb, r1.yonsan, :old.aek, w_msg);
        if  w_msg != 'Y'    then raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
        end if;
    end loop;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.dt_skt0bu_i
    after insert on skt0bu
    for each row
------------------------------------
-- 분개장 총계정원장 갱신 trigger --
------------------------------------
declare
    w_msg                       varchar(200);
begin
    ----------------------------------------------
    --  해당계정의 총계정원장  차대변금액 갱신  --
    ----------------------------------------------
    SR_UPDAG (:new.corp_gr, :new.tr_ymd, :new.work_gb, :new.fund_cd, :new.fund_currency, :new.gwamok, :new.chadae_gb, 1, :new.aek, w_msg);
    if  w_msg != 'Y'    then raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
    end if;
    ---------------------------------------------
    --  합산계정의 총계정원장 차대변금액 갱신  --
    ---------------------------------------------
    for r1 in (
        SELECT  hab_gwamok
              , yonsan
        FROM    szx0cc t1
        WHERE   gwamok = :new.gwamok
    ) loop
        SR_UPDAG (:new.corp_gr, :new.tr_ymd, :new.work_gb, :new.fund_cd, :new.fund_currency, r1.hab_gwamok, :new.chadae_gb, r1.yonsan, :new.aek, w_msg);
        if  w_msg != 'Y'    then raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
        end if;
    end loop;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.dt_skt0bu_u
    after update on skt0bu for each row
----------------------------------------------
-- 분개장(update시) 총계정원장 갱신 trigger --
----------------------------------------------
declare
    w_msg                       varchar(200);
begin
    ----------------------------------------------
    --  해당계정의 총계정원장  차대변금액 갱신  --
    ----------------------------------------------
    -- 기존계정 차감
    SR_UPDAG (:old.corp_gr, :old.tr_ymd, :old.work_gb, :old.fund_cd, :old.fund_currency, :old.gwamok, :old.chadae_gb, -1, nvl(:old.aek, 0), w_msg);
    if  w_msg != 'Y'    then raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
    end if;
    -- 새로운계정 update
    SR_UPDAG (:new.corp_gr, :new.tr_ymd, :new.work_gb, :new.fund_cd, :new.fund_currency, :new.gwamok, :new.chadae_gb, 1, nvl(:new.aek, 0), w_msg);
    if  w_msg != 'Y'    then raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
    end if;

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    --  합산계정의 총계정원장 차대변금액 갱신(기존계정 차감)  --
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    for r1 in (
        SELECT  hab_gwamok
              , yonsan * -1  AS yonsan
        FROM    szx0cc t1
        WHERE   gwamok = :old.gwamok
    ) loop
        SR_UPDAG (:old.corp_gr, :old.tr_ymd, :old.work_gb, :old.fund_cd, :old.fund_currency, r1.hab_gwamok, :old.chadae_gb, r1.yonsan, nvl(:old.aek, 0), w_msg);
        if  w_msg != 'Y'    then raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
        end if;
    end loop;
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    --  합산계정의 총계정원장 차대변금액 갱신(새로운계정 update)  --
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    for r2 in (
        SELECT  hab_gwamok
              , yonsan
        FROM    szx0cc t1
        WHERE   gwamok = :new.gwamok
    ) loop
        SR_UPDAG (:new.corp_gr, :new.tr_ymd, :new.work_gb, :new.fund_cd, :new.fund_currency, r2.hab_gwamok, :new.chadae_gb, r2.yonsan, nvl(:new.aek, 0), w_msg);
        if  w_msg != 'Y'    then raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
        end if;
    end loop;
END;
/

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SKT0BU.CORP_GR IS '운용(자문)사';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SKT0BU.TR_YMD IS '분개일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SKT0BU.WORK_GB IS '결산구분(A:결산전 B:결산)';

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SKT0BU.UPMU_GB IS '증권사구분(1:국내 2:해외)';

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.DT_SRS_CD_U  
    AFTER UPDATE 
    ON SRS_CD FOR EACH ROW 
--------------------------------
-- 해외 파생정보 갱신 trigger --
--------------------------------
DECLARE
BEGIN
--    WHEN MATCHED THEN
--         UPDATE
--            SET BALH_NATION     = mm.BALH_NATION
--              , CURRENCY        = mm.CURRENCY
--              , SANGJ_YMD       = TO_DATE (:old.TRD_FR_DATE,'yyyymmdd')
--              , ISIN_CD         = :old.SRS_CD
--              , LSY_YMD         = TO_DATE (:old.EXPR_DATE,'yyyymmdd')
--              , HANGSA_GA       = :old.STKPRC
--              , STOCK_GB        = mm.STOCK_GB
--              , SJ_GB           = :old.sj_gb
--              , JASAN           = :old.ISIN_CD
--              , JASAN_ATTR      = 'A13'
--              , CHANGE_CURRENCY = mm.CURRENCY
--              , CHANGE_JM_CD    = :old.ISIN_CD
--              , EXCD            = :old.EXCH_CD
--              , UNIT_AEK        = 100
--              , IU_DT           = SYSDATE

    MERGE INTO SYM0YA t1  /* 해외 자산종목 */
       USING ( SELECT ta.CORP_GR
                    , ta.CURRENCY
                    , ta.BALH_NATION
                    , ta.STOCK_GB
                 FROM SYM0YA ta  /* 해외 자산종목 */
                WHERE ta.CORP_GR  LIKE '%'
                  AND ta.JM_CD    = :old.ISIN_CD
                  AND ta.JASAN_GB = '1' ) mm 
          ON ( t1.JM_CD = :old.SRS_CD )
    WHEN MATCHED THEN
         UPDATE
            SET IU_DT = SYSDATE
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
         INSERT ( CORP_GR          /* _1- */
                , JM_CD            /* _2- */
                , JM_NM            /* _3- */
                , JASAN_GB         /* _4- */
                , SEQ_NO           /* _5- */
                , BALH_NATION      /* _6- */
                , CURRENCY         /* _7- */
                , SANGJ_YMD        /* _8- */
                , ISIN_CD          /* _9- */
                , LSY_YMD          /* _10- */
                , UNIT_AEK         /* _11- */
                , HANGSA_GA        /* _12- */
                , STOCK_GB         /* _13- */
                , SJ_GB            /* _14- */
                , JASAN            /* _15- */
                , JASAN_ATTR       /* _16- */
                , CHANGE_CURRENCY  /* _17- */
                , CHANGE_JM_CD     /* _18- */
                , EXCD             /* _19- */
                , IU_USER          /* _20- */
                , IU_DT            /* _21- */
                )
         VALUES ( mm.CORP_GR                            /* _1- */
                , :old.SRS_CD                           /* _2- */
                , :old.JM_NM                            /* _3- */
                , 'A'                                   /* _4- */
                , 1                                     /* _5- */
                , mm.BALH_NATION                        /* _6- */
                , mm.CURRENCY                           /* _7- */
                , TO_DATE(:old.TRD_FR_DATE,'yyyymmdd')  /* _8- */
                , :old.SRS_CD                           /* _9- */
                , TO_DATE(:old.EXPR_DATE,'yyyymmdd')    /* _10- */
                , 100                                   /* _11- */
                , :old.STKPRC                           /* _12- */
                , mm.STOCK_GB                           /* _13- */
                , :old.SJ_GB                            /* _14- */
                , :old.ISIN_CD                          /* _15- */
                , 'A13'                                 /* _16- */
                , mm.CURRENCY                           /* _17- */
                , :old.ISIN_CD                          /* _18- */
                , :old.EXCH_CD                          /* _19- */
                , 'DT_SRS_CD_U'                         /* _20- */
                , SYSDATE                               /* _21- */
                );
END;
/

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.SRS_CD IS '해외 CALL, PUT 종목코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.STKPRC IS '행사가격';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.BLBG_TCKR IS '블름버그 티커';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.ISIN_CD IS '현물 표준코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.EXCH_CD IS '거래소';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.CRC_CD IS '통화코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.EXPR_DATE IS '옵션만기일';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.CRE_DT IS '생성일시';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.WEEK IS '년(yy) + 월 + 주(1-5)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.JM_NM IS '종목명';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SRS_CD.SJ_GB IS '2-CALL, 3-PUT';

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER kfp.dt_sst0kt_d
    after delete on sst0kt
    for each row WHEN (old.tr_cd not like 'L%')
declare
    w_msg                       varchar(100);
begin
    SR_UPDKM9 (:old.corp_gr, :old.tr_ymd, :old.fund_cd, :old.tr_cd, :old.sj_cd, :old.chasu, :old.gyey_su * -1, :old.tr_co_cd, 'd', w_msg);
    if  w_msg != 'Y'    then    raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER KFP.dt_sst0kt_i
    after insert on sst0kt
    for each row WHEN (new.tr_cd not like 'L%')
declare
    w_msg                       varchar(200);
begin
    SR_UPDKM9 ( :new.corp_gr, :new.tr_ymd, :new.fund_cd, :new.tr_cd, :new.sj_cd, :new.chasu
              , :new.gyey_su, :new.tr_co_cd, 'i', w_msg );
    if  w_msg != 'Y'    then    raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
    end if;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER kfp.dt_sst0kt_u
    after update on sst0kt for each row
--
-- 선물거래 수정 trigger
--
declare
    w_msg                       varchar(200);
begin
    SR_UPDKM9 (:old.corp_gr, :old.tr_ymd, :old.fund_cd, :old.tr_cd, :old.sj_cd, :old.chasu, - :old.gyey_su, :old.tr_co_cd, 'u', w_msg);
    if  w_msg != 'Y'    then    raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
    end if;

    SR_UPDKM9 (:new.corp_gr, :new.tr_ymd, :new.fund_cd, :new.tr_cd, :new.sj_cd, :new.chasu, :new.gyey_su, :new.tr_co_cd, 'u', w_msg);
    if  w_msg != 'Y'    then    raise_application_error (-20301, w_msg);
    end if;
END;
/

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.SST0KT IS '파생 거래내역';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SST0KT.CORP_GR IS '운용(자문)사';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SST0KT.TR_YMD IS '매매일';

CREATE TABLE KFP.SSX0KJ
(
    CORP_GR     VARCHAR2(8) NOT NULL,
    SJ_CD       VARCHAR2(12) NOT NULL,
    SJ_NM       VARCHAR2(160),
    SJ_GB       VARCHAR2(1),
    LSY_YMD     DATE,
    UNIT_AEK    NUMBER,
    HANGSA_GA   NUMBER(11,2),
    JASAN       VARCHAR2(3 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    KSD_SJ_CD   VARCHAR2(14),

(
    CORP_GR             VARCHAR2(8) NOT NULL,
    TR_YMD              DATE NOT NULL,
    TR_CD               VARCHAR2(3) NOT NULL,
    FUND_CD             VARCHAR2(12) NOT NULL,
    TR_CO_CD            VARCHAR2(5) NOT NULL,
    TRUSTEE             VARCHAR2(5) NOT NULL,
    JM_CD               VARCHAR2(30) NOT NULL,
    CURRENCY            VARCHAR2(3),
    TR_SEQ              NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TR_JUSU             NUMBER(17,4),
    TR_DANGA            NUMBER(17,4),
    TR_TAX              NUMBER(17,4),
    TR_COST             NUMBER(17,4),
    TR_AEK              NUMBER(17,4),
    GYULJE_YMD          DATE,
    GYULJE_AEK          NUMBER(17,4),
    WON_GYULJE_AEK      NUMBER(13),
    CHUI_AEK            NUMBER(17,4),
    WON_CHUI_AEK        NUMBER(13),
    WON_TR_SONIK        NUMBER(13),
    WON_HWAN_CHA        NUMBER(13),
    GYULJE_JM_CD        VARCHAR2(12),
    COUNT_COST          VARCHAR2(5),
    SEC_CODE            VARCHAR2(10),
    SETT_LOCATION       VARCHAR2(11),
    COUNT_BIC           VARCHAR2(11),
    TRANS_RT            NUMBER,
    UPD_USER            VARCHAR2(40),
    PASS_GB             VARCHAR2(1),
    MK_SUIK_PER         NUMBER,
    PASS_IJA            NUMBER,
    WON_PASS_IJA        NUMBER,
    MISU_IJA            NUMBER,
    WON_MISU_IJA        NUMBER,
    WON_TAX_AEK         NUMBER,
    BS_TYPE             NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    FCUR_GYULJE_AEK     NUMBER,
    FCUR_SONIK          NUMBER,
    FCUR_HWAN_CHA       NUMBER,
    FCUR_CHUI_AEK       NUMBER,
    FCUR_PASS_IJA       NUMBER,
    FCUR_MISU_IJA       NUMBER,
    TR_HALIN_AEK        NUMBER,
    WON_TR_HALIN_AEK    NUMBER,
    FCUR_TR_HALIN_AEK   NUMBER,
    DX_JM_CD            VARCHAR2(12),
    TODAY_IJA           NUMBER,
    WON_TODAY_IJA       NUMBER
)

STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.YMD IS '처리일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.TR_YMD IS '거래발생일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.TR_CD IS '거래코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.FUND_CD IS '펀드코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.TR_SEQ IS '거래순번';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.TR_CO_CD IS '매매처코드';

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.TRUSTEE IS '거래소(해외보관처)';

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.REQUEST_YMD IS '신청일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.REQUEST_AEK IS '신청금액';

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.GWA_AEK IS '과표액';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.WON_GWA_AEK IS '과표액(원화,거래발생일환율)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.TAX_AEK IS '현지납부세액';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.WON_TAX_AEK IS '현지납부세액(원화,거래발생일환율)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.GROSS_UP_RT IS 'GROSS UP 세율';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.GROSS_UP_TAX IS 'GROSS UP 세액(원화) = TRUNC (과표액(원화) * GROSS UP 세율,0)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SYT0TAX.GROSS_UP_AEK IS 'GROSS UP 환급세액 = LEAST (현지납부세액(원화), GROSS UP 세액(원화))';

GRANT INSERT ON KFP.SZX9DT TO AAMS;
GRANT UPDATE ON KFP.SZX9DT TO AAMS;
GRANT SELECT ON KFP.SZX9DT TO AAMS;

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.SZX9DT IS '작업일자 Between조건용 일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.SZX9DT.YMD IS '일자';

CREATE SYNONYM AAMS.SZX9DT
FOR KFP.SZX9DT;

CREATE TABLE KFP.TABLE_CONV
(
    TABLE_NM        VARCHAR2(40) NOT NULL,
    TABLE_SEQ       NUMBER(5) NOT NULL,
    SELECT_SQL      CLOB,
    HEADER_NM       CLOB,
    CMNT            VARCHAR2(2000),
    SELECT_TABLE    VARCHAR2(400),
    FILE_COPY       VARCHAR2(1) DEFAULT '0',
    SEND_GROUP      VARCHAR2(5) DEFAULT 'H2O01'
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS
LOB (HEADER_NM) STORE AS BASICFILE 
(
    STORAGE
    (
        INITIAL 64K
        NEXT 1M
    )
    CHUNK 8192
    RETENTION
)
LOB (SELECT_SQL) STORE AS BASICFILE 
(
    STORAGE
    (
        INITIAL 64K
        NEXT 1M
    )
    CHUNK 8192
    RETENTION
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.TABLE_CONV_PK
ON KFP.TABLE_CONV (TABLE_NM,TABLE_SEQ) 
TABLESPACE INDEX_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.TABLE_CONV IS '테이블 자료 교환용 Dynamic SQL(H2O OMS 등)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.TABLE_CONV.TABLE_NM IS '자료 교환용 테이블 명';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.TABLE_CONV.TABLE_SEQ IS '순번';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.TABLE_CONV.SELECT_SQL IS 'SELECT문';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.TABLE_CONV.HEADER_NM IS 'Header(구분자 @)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.TABLE_CONV.CMNT IS '상세설명';

    PM5                     NUMBER,
    VAR5                    NUMBER,

    PM10                    NUMBER,
    VAR10                   NUMBER,
    AM15                    NUMBER,
    PM15                    NUMBER,
    VAR15                   NUMBER,

    PM20                    NUMBER,
    VAR20                   NUMBER,

    PM60                    NUMBER,
    VAR60                   NUMBER,
    AM120                   NUMBER,
    PM120                   NUMBER,
    VAR120                  NUMBER,

    G30_VOLATILITY          NUMBER,

    GIJUN_PRICE             NUMBER,

    OVRS_EXCG_CD            VARCHAR2(4),

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM.PM IS 'price of market';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM.VAR IS 'VAR';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM.D30_VOLATILITY IS '30일기준 단기변동성(변동성 추이 확인용)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM.G30_VOLATILITY IS '30일기준 변동성';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM.TT_VOLATILITY IS '기준변동성';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM.SHORT_PRICE IS '단기평균가격';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM.GIJUN_PRICE IS '기준평균가격';

    NEXT 1M
);

COMMENT ON TABLE KFP.USPM_DD IS 'US Portfolio Model 종목 일별 기준변동성 현황';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM_DD.CORP_GR IS '운용(자문)사';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM_DD.YMD IS '기준일자';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM_DD.JM_CD IS '종목코드';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM_DD.JONGA IS '기준일종가';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM_DD.D30_VOLATILITY IS '30일기준 단기변동성(변동성 추이 확인용) = (sqrt (2주VAR * 30) * 0.6) + (sqrt (1개월VAR * 30) * 0.4)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM_DD.G30_VOLATILITY IS '30일기준 변동성 = (sqrt (1개월VAR * 30) * 0.4) + (sqrt (3개월VAR * 30) * 0.3) + (sqrt (6개월VAR * 30) * 0.2) + (sqrt (1년VAR * 30) * 0.1)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM_DD.SHORT_PRICE IS '단기평균가격 = (2주평균 * 0.6) + (1개월평균 * 0.4)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM_DD.GIJUN_PRICE IS '기준평균가격 = (2주평균 * 0.4) + (3개얼평균 * 0.3) + (6개월평균 * 0.2) + (1년평균 * 0.1)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.USPM_DD.COM_DT IS '계산일시';

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.XFW_USER.POS_NM IS '직위명';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.XFW_USER.TEL_NO IS '전화번호';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.XFW_USER.EMAIL IS '이메일';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.XFW_USER.START_YMD IS '사용시작일';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.XFW_USER.CLOSE_YMD IS '사용종료일';
COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.XFW_USER.USER_ROLE_CD IS '사용자구분코드';

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.XFW_USER.LOGIN_ID IS '로그인아이디';

COMMENT ON COLUMN KFP.XFW_USER.NOTE IS '비고';

CREATE TABLE KFP.XFW_USER_BACK
(
    USER_ID         NUMBER NOT NULL,
    COMPANY_ID      NUMBER NOT NULL,
    USER_NM         VARCHAR2(100) NOT NULL,
    DEPT_NM         VARCHAR2(100),
    POS_NM          VARCHAR2(20),
    TEL_NO          VARCHAR2(20),
    EMAIL           VARCHAR2(100),
    START_YMD       DATE NOT NULL,
    CLOSE_YMD       DATE,
    USER_ROLE_CD    VARCHAR2(10),
    LOGIN_ALLOW_YN  VARCHAR2(10) NOT NULL,
    LOGIN_ID        VARCHAR2(20) NOT NULL,
    LOGIN_PW        VARCHAR2(100) NOT NULL,
    NOTE            VARCHAR2(1000)
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;


CREATE TABLE KFP.XFW_USER_MENU
(
    USER_MENU_ID    NUMBER NOT NULL,
    CORP_GR         VARCHAR2(10) NOT NULL,
    USER_ID         VARCHAR2(10) NOT NULL,
    MENU_ID         NUMBER
)
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
)
NOCOMPRESS;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.XFW_USER_MENU_CORP_GR_IDX
ON KFP.XFW_USER_MENU (CORP_GR,USER_ID,MENU_ID) 
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX KFP.XFW_USER_MENU_USER_MENU_ID_IDX
ON KFP.XFW_USER_MENU (USER_MENU_ID) 
TABLESPACE DATA_KFP
STORAGE
(
    INITIAL 64K
    NEXT 1M
);
